# 附录 2 · 复习索引

| Day | 中英表达对比点 |
|---|---|
| D61 | 「我负责……」——I'm responsible for + 名词 / -ing |
| D61 | 「我的工作是……」——My main job is to… / My main job is -ing |
| D61 | 「主要」——main（形容词）vs mainly（副词） |
| D61 | 「我顶替她」——I'm covering for her / I'm taking over |
| D62 | 「先……然后……再……最后」——To begin with / Next / Then / Once that's done / Finally |
| D62 | 「上传」「提交」——upload sth to… / submit |
| D62 | 「需要……」——It requires… / You need to…（不说 It needs you to…） |
| D62 | 「一……就……」——once + 一般现在时 |
| D63 | 「订单发出去了」——The order has been shipped.（东西做主语 → 被动） |
| D63 | 被动的时态——时态变在 be 上：is / was / has been / will be |
| D63 | 「系统自动生成」——It's generated automatically. |
| D63 | 被动句什么时候加 by…？——只在「谁做的」是重要信息时 |
| D64 | 「这个归谁管？」——Who handles this? / Who should I talk to about…? |
| D64 | 「我把他拉进来」——I'll loop him in. / I'll cc him.（不说 pull him in） |
| D64 | 「我回头答复你」——I'll get back to you.（不说 I'll answer you later） |
| D64 | 用被动说分工——Refunds are handled by the finance team. |
| D65 | 开头先说目的——I'm writing to… |
| D65 | 「麻烦您……」——Could you please…? |
| D65 | 「具体如下」——Please see below. / the following |
| D65 | 结尾——一句话 + Best regards,（不堆砌客套） |
| D66 | 「你的意思是……吗？」——Do you mean…? / So what you're saying is… |
| D66 | 「我确认一下」——Just to confirm, …? |
| D66 | 「换句话说」——In other words, … |
| D66 | 「我以为」I thought… vs「我先这样理解」I assume… |
| D67 | 「负责」「归……管」——be responsible for / in charge of / handle（复习 D61、D64；第一册 D4） |
| D67 | 「订单发了」——is shipped / has been shipped vs ships（复习 D63、D64） |
| D67 | 「主要」——main（+ 名词）vs mainly（+ 动词或整句）（复习 D61） |
| D67 | 「你是说……吗？」vs「……是什么意思？」——Do you mean…? vs What does … mean?（复习 D66；第一册 D13） |
| D67 | 「我以为……」I thought… vs「我想（还没确认）……」I assume…（复习 D66） |
| D68 | 「120 万」「1 亿」——英文三位一组：one point two million / one hundred million |
| D68 | 小数：6.5 = six point five；0.75 = zero point seven five |
| D68 | 「提高了 2 个百分点」——two percentage points（≠ two percent） |
| D68 | 分数和「……中的……」——a third / three quarters / ninety-four out of a hundred |
| D69 | increase by（增加了多少）vs increase to（增加到多少） |
| D69 | 「下降」——fall / drop / decline，「让它下降」reduce |
| D69 | 程度副词——sharply / slightly / dramatically / significantly |
| D69 | 「同比」year over year；「环比」month over month |
| D70 | 「占……%」——account for / make up（不说 occupy） |
| D70 | 「如图所示」——As you can see, … |
| D70 | 「大多数包裹」——the majority of + 复数名词 + 复数动词 |
| D70 | 「图」和「表」——chart / graph / table |
| D71 | 汇报先说结论——Overall, … / In short, … |
| D71 | 「这周解决了 18 张」——We've resolved… vs Last week, we resolved… |
| D71 | 「还在弄」——ongoing / pending / on hold / overdue |
| D71 | 「有点悬」「卡住了」——at risk / The blocker is… / blocked by… |
| D72 | 「因为」——because + 句子 vs because of / due to + 名词 |
| D72 | 「导致」——lead to / cause / result in（不说 make … happen） |
| D72 | 「影响」——affect（动词）/ effect（名词）/ impact |
| D72 | 「所以」——as a result / so / that's why |
| D73 | 答不上来——I'm not sure off the top of my head. Let me check and get back to you. |
| D73 | 「大概」——about / around / I'd say / a ballpark / in the range of |
| D73 | 「我觉得」有多确定——definitely / I believe / possibly / I doubt |
| D73 | 「会后再说」——Can we take this offline? |
| D74 | 「涨了多少」「涨到多少」——increase by / increase to；percent / percentage points（复习 D68、D69） |
| D74 | 「因为」——because + 句子 vs because of / due to + 名词（复习 D72） |
| D74 | 「影响」——affect（动词）vs effect（名词）（复习 D72） |
| D74 | 「占……%」——account for / make up vs occupy（复习 D70） |
| D74 | 「你确定吗？」——I'm sure vs I believe（复习 D73） |
| D75 | 回复咨询——先感谢、直接回答，再给细节 |
| D75 | 「目前还不支持」——We don't support … at the moment. |
| D75 | 「有问题随时联系我」——Feel free to contact me. |
| D75 | 「免费试用」——a free trial / free of charge |
| D76 | 条件——as long as / if / unless |
| D76 | 「不允许」「必须」——be not allowed to / be required to |
| D76 | 「根据我们的规定」——According to our policy, … |
| D76 | 「一般情况下」——Normally, … / In general, … |
| D77 | 道歉的三个级别——Sorry about that. / I apologize for… / We sincerely apologize for… |
| D77 | 道歉之后——一定要说「我们在做什么」 |
| D77 | 「这是我们的问题」——This is on us. / We take full responsibility. |
| D77 | 「以后不会再发生」——不轻易说 never |
| D78 | 「这个做不了」——理解 + 原因 + 替代方案 |
| D78 | 拒绝的开头——I'm afraid… / Unfortunately… |
| D78 | 「我们会考虑」——We'll consider it. ≠ 答应了 |
| D78 | 「我尽量」——I'll do my best, but I can't promise… |
| D79 | 「您先别激动」——不说 Calm down!，说 I hear you. |
| D79 | 「我理解得对吗？」——So, if I understand correctly, … |
| D79 | 「我尽快回复您」——给出时间：I'll call you back by three o'clock. |
| D79 | 「这不是我的错」——不说出口，只说能做什么 |
| D80 | 「催一下」——不说 You didn't reply，说 Just following up on… |
| D80 | 「不知道您……」——I was wondering if… |
| D80 | 「您方便的时候」——when you get a chance（别用 Kindly…ASAP） |
| D80 | 给对方台阶——In case it got buried in your inbox, … |
| D81 | 「恐怕不行」——I'm afraid not / I'm afraid we can't… vs Unfortunately, …（复习 D76、D78） |
| D81 | 「只要……就」vs「除非……否则」——as long as vs unless（复习 D76） |
| D81 | 「很抱歉」——I'm sorry to hear about… vs I apologize for…（复习 D27、D77） |
| D81 | 「我们会考虑」——consider ≠ agree（复习 D78） |
| D81 | 「催一下」——Just following up on… vs You didn't reply（复习 D80） |
| D82 | 「我没有权限」——I don't have access to… / I don't have permission to… |
| D82 | 「给他开个权限」——Could you give Leo access to…? |
| D82 | 「权限被收回了」——His access was removed / revoked. |
| D82 | admin、user、read-only——管理员、普通用户和「只读」 |
| D83 | 「对接」——integrate with / connect to（不说 dock） |
| D83 | 「同步」——sync / in sync |
| D83 | 「上线」——go live / launch（不说 go online） |
| D83 | 讲技术用比喻——An API is like a waiter between two systems. |
| D84 | 「系统崩了」——说具体：It crashed. / It's down. / The page isn't loading. |
| D84 | 「老是出错」——It keeps failing. / every time / intermittent |
| D84 | 报 bug 四要素——what I did / what I expected / what actually happened / how often |
| D84 | 「我这边没问题」——It works fine on my end. |
| D85 | 「被海关扣了」——It's being held at customs. |
| D85 | 「派送失败」——The delivery attempt failed. |
| D85 | 美国地址：从小到大写——unit / suite / apt、zip code |
| D85 | 「退回发件人」——It was returned to sender. |
| D86 | 事故经过：过去时 + 被动——The outage was detected at 2:10 p.m. |
| D86 | 「截至目前」——As of now, … |
| D86 | 「今后」——Going forward, we'll … |
| D86 | 「影响了 2% 的订单」——About two percent of orders were affected. |
| D87 | 「下班前给你」——by end of day，还要说清是谁的时区 |
| D87 | 「明天一早」——first thing tomorrow morning |
| D87 | 「对齐一下」——make sure we're on the same page |
| D87 | 「提前跟你打个招呼」——give sb a heads-up |
| D88 | 「质量问题」——defective / faulty，不说 quality problem |
| D88 | 「七天无理由退货」——a 7-day no-questions-asked return policy |
| D88 | 「扣钱」——deduct a fee from the refund |
| D88 | 「不再……」——no longer + 动词 |
| D89 | 介绍工作——一般现在时 + 被动：Every order is checked… |
| D89 | 「欢迎来到……」——Welcome to… / It's a pleasure to have you here. |
| D89 | 「终于见到本人了」——It's great to finally meet you in person. |
| D89 | 「我带您过一遍」——Let me walk you through… |
| D90 | 「订单发了」「故障两点十分发现的」——被动 vs 主动（复习 D63、D64、D85、D86、D89） |
| D90 | 「涨了三个点」「涨到 96%」——percent vs percentage point；increase by vs to（复习 D68、D69、D71） |
| D90 | 「因为罢工」「对客户影响很大」——because / because of / due to；affect / effect（复习 D72） |
| D90 | 「这个做不了」「只要……就……」「除非……」——I'm afraid we can't… vs No；as long as / unless（复习 D76、D78、D79） |
| D90 | 「我没有权限」——access vs permission（复习 D82） |

| Day | 核心表达 | 什么时候用 |
|---|---|---|
| D61 | I'm responsible for + 名词 / -ing | 一句话说清自己的职责范围 |
| D61 | My main job is to… | 概括工作的核心 |
| D61 | I'm their point of contact. | 告诉对方「有事找我」 |
| D61 | I check … on a daily basis. | 说日常工作和频率 |
| D61 | I mainly + 动词 | 说主要做什么 |
| D61 | If there's a big problem, I escalate it to… | 说遇到大问题交给谁 |
| D61 | What does your job involve? / Can you give me a quick overview? | 请别人介绍工作或项目 |
| D61 | I'm covering for… / I'm taking over… | 临时顶班 vs 正式接手 |
| D62 | To begin with, … | 讲流程的第一步 |
| D62 | Next, + 祈使句 / Then we… | 讲中间的步骤 |
| D62 | Once that's done, … | 承上启下：这一步完成后 |
| D62 | Finally, … | 讲最后一步 |
| D62 | Let's go through it step by step. | 带人从头到尾过一遍 |
| D62 | Upload … to … / Submit … | 说系统操作 |
| D62 | … needs / requires … ／ You need to … | 说规定和要求 |
| D62 | Could you send over …? | 请对方把东西发过来 |
| D63 | The order has been shipped. | 告诉对方一件事已经完成 |
| D63 | Once an order is placed, … | 讲流程的起点和条件 |
| D63 | … is created / generated automatically. | 说系统自动完成的事 |
| D63 | The package is picked up from … | 说东西被取走、运走 |
| D63 | It's sorted and loaded onto a truck. | 一句话连说两个被动动作 |
| D63 | Your customer is notified at every step. | 说对方会收到通知 |
| D63 | … on behalf of your store | 说以谁的名义发出或去做 |
| D63 | … by hand / manually | 说人工完成 |
| D64 | Who handles …? / Who should I talk to about …? | 问「这事归谁管」 |
| D64 | … are handled by … | 用被动说分工 |
| D64 | I'll loop … in. / I've cc'd … | 把同事拉进邮件或对话 |
| D64 | Let me find the right person and get back to you. | 自己答不了，先稳住对方 |
| D64 | I'll get back to you by … | 承诺回复的时间 |
| D64 | … deals with … | 说某人负责处理哪类事 |
| D64 | Keep it in one thread and cc the relevant people. | 邮件礼仪：不另起新邮件 |
| D64 | Feel free to reach out to me directly. | 邮件结尾：有事直接找我 |
| D65 | I'm writing to + 动词… | 邮件第一句说目的 |
| D65 | As mentioned in my last email, … | 提醒对方之前说过的信息 |
| D65 | Please see below for the details. | 引出下面的要点或表格 |
| D65 | To clarify, … | 补充说明，提前避免误会 |
| D65 | Could you please … at your earliest convenience? | 礼貌地请对方尽快做一件事 |
| D65 | For your reference, I've attached… | 附上参考资料 |
| D65 | Let me know if you have any questions regarding… | 一句话收尾 |
| D65 | Best regards, + 名字 | 邮件最后一行 |
| D66 | Do you mean…? | 猜到对方的意思后求证 |
| D66 | Sorry, what do you mean by "…"? | 问某个说法具体指什么 |
| D66 | Can you give me some context? | 请对方说说来龙去脉 |
| D66 | In other words, … | 换一种说法，把意思说得更直白 |
| D66 | So what you're saying is… | 用自己的话复述对方的意思 |
| D66 | Just to confirm, …? | 核对时间、数字、范围 |
| D66 | I assume…, so I'll… | 把自己的推测说出来，让对方纠正 |
| D66 | I want to get it right the first time. | 说明你为什么多问几句 |
| D67 | I'm writing to + 动词… | 来自 D65：邮件第一句就说目的 |
| D67 | In my absence, … is my backup. / I've delegated … to … | 来自 D61：说清楚谁临时替你、交出去哪些工作（cover for = 临时顶替，take over = 正式接手） |
| D67 | I'm responsible for… / … are handled by… / Loop … in. | 来自 D61、D64：说自己的职责和别人的分工 |
| D67 | To begin with, … Next, … Once that's done, … Finally, … | 来自 D62：按顺序讲清一个流程 |
| D67 | The order has been shipped. / Once it's approved, … | 来自 D63：用被动说东西的状态和流程，by… 只在「谁做的」是重点时才加 |
| D67 | Just to confirm, …? / Do you mean…? / I assume…? | 来自 D66：把模糊的要求和自己的推测问清楚 |
| D68 | We shipped + 数字 + packages last month. | 报总量 |
| D68 | The exact figure is… / That's nearly… / roughly… | 先给精确数，再给好记的约数 |
| D68 | On average, … takes… | 报平均值 |
| D68 | … out of … | 用「几个里有几个」说比例 |
| D68 | That's roughly a third of… | 用分数说占比 |
| D68 | … went up / down by … percentage points | 比较两个百分比 |
| D68 | at least … / up to … | 说下限和上限 |
| D68 | A minus B equals C. | 当场把账算给别人听 |
| D69 | … increased by + 变化量 | 说变化的幅度 |
| D69 | … increased to + 新数值 | 说变化后的新数值 |
| D69 | Year over year, … | 和去年同期比 |
| D69 | … dropped sharply / rose slightly | 说变化的方向和程度 |
| D69 | … have remained stable. / … has stayed flat. | 说数据一直没变 |
| D69 | … fluctuate between A and B. | 说数据忽高忽低 |
| D69 | X reduced / boosted Y. | 说某个做法带来了什么变化 |
| D69 | We hit a record high. | 宣布创了新高 |
| D70 | As you can see, this chart shows… | 讲图时的第一句：先说这张图是什么 |
| D70 | The blue bars represent… | 解释颜色、线条代表什么 |
| D70 | The horizontal axis shows…, and the vertical axis shows… | 解释坐标轴 |
| D70 | … accounts for / makes up … percent of the total. | 说占比 |
| D70 | The majority of … / The rest of … | 说「大多数」「其余的」 |
| D70 | What's the source of the data? — It's from… | 问、答数据来源 |
| D70 | Sorry, thirteen or thirty? — Thirteen, one-three. | 确认容易听错的数字 |
| D70 | I've highlighted … in red. | 引导大家看重点 |
| D71 | Overall, it was a good week. / In short, … | 周报和汇报的第一句：先给结论 |
| D71 | We achieved all three of our KPIs. | 说指标完成情况 |
| D71 | We've resolved … of the … tickets. … are still pending. | 说已完成和待处理的数量 |
| D71 | … is a day overdue, so I've escalated it to… | 说超期，并说明自己已经做了什么 |
| D71 | … is at risk. The main blocker is… | 提前说风险和卡点 |
| D71 | … is ahead of schedule / ongoing / on hold until… | 说其他项目的状态 |
| D71 | Here are the next steps. My action item is… | 结尾：下一步谁做什么、什么时候 |
| D72 | In short, … | 解释原因之前，先给一句结论 |
| D72 | … led to … | 说「A 导致 B」 |
| D72 | As a result, … | 说因果链的下一环 |
| D72 | … went up / down due to … | 正式地说原因 |
| D72 | … was partly / largely caused by … | 说原因占多大比重 |
| D72 | How did this affect …? — It had a big impact on … | 问和答影响 |
| D72 | Supply couldn't keep up with demand. | 说供不应求 |
| D73 | That's a good question. | 被提问时先接住问题，争取几秒钟 |
| D73 | I'm not sure off the top of my head. | 坦白手边没有答案 |
| D73 | I'd say around … | 给出一个估计 |
| D73 | Somewhere in the range of … to … | 给一个估计区间 |
| D73 | Let me figure out … and get back to you. | 承诺会后查清楚再回复 |
| D73 | I believe … / I doubt … / It's unlikely that … | 说出自己有多确定 |
| D73 | That's a fair question. Can we take this offline? | 太细、太技术的问题约到会后 |
| D74 | Overall, it was a strong quarter, with one problem. | 来自 D71：汇报第一句先给结论，再说有没有问题 |
| D74 | … increased by … . They increased to … . / That's up … percentage points. | 来自 D68、D69：说清变化量、新数值和百分点 |
| D74 | As you can see, … accounts for / makes up … percent of the total. | 来自 D70：讲图表、说占比（不说 occupy） |
| D74 | … due to … / Because of …, … / …, because + 句子 | 来自 D72：说原因，分清后面接名词还是句子 |
| D74 | Did … affect …? — It had very little / a big effect on … | 来自 D72：问和答影响 |
| D74 | I believe so. / I'd say …, but I'm not certain yet. / Let me check and get back to you. | 来自 D73：被追问时说清自己有多确定，答不上来就承诺跟进 |
| D75 | Thanks for your interest in … | 回复咨询的第一句 |
| D75 | The short answer is yes / not yet. | 第二句就给答案，再展开 |
| D75 | We don't support … at the moment. | 说「暂不支持」，暗示以后会有 |
| D75 | We're planning to … next year. | 说计划，让客户知道以后会有 |
| D75 | In the meantime, you can … | 给一个现在就能用的过渡方案 |
| D75 | You can try … free of charge. | 免费试用、免费服务 |
| D75 | I'd be happy to give you a quick demo. | 主动提出下一步 |
| D75 | Feel free to contact me if you have any questions. | 邮件结尾，请对方随时联系 |
| D76 | According to our policy, … | 引用公司规定，说明「这不是我个人的决定」 |
| D76 | … are eligible for … | 说什么情况能赔、能享受 |
| D76 | You're required to … within … | 说必须做的事和期限 |
| D76 | As long as you …, we'll … | 说「只要……就……」，给对方希望 |
| D76 | Unless …, the maximum is … | 说例外和上限 |
| D76 | We're not allowed to … | 说「规定不允许」，不是我不愿意 |
| D76 | Normally, … | 说通常的做法（暗示有例外） |
| D76 | In that case, I'm afraid we can't make an exception. | 根据对方的情况，礼貌而明确地拒绝破例 |
| D77 | We sincerely apologize for … | 以公司名义正式道歉 |
| D77 | I completely understand your frustration. | 先接住对方的情绪，再说事 |
| D77 | We take full responsibility for the mistake. | 承认是我们的错 |
| D77 | The root cause was … | 一句话说清根本原因 |
| D77 | Here's how we'll make it right. | 引出具体的补救办法 |
| D77 | As a goodwill gesture, we've added … | 规定之外主动补偿 |
| D77 | Lost packages will be reshipped, and late ones will be expedited. | 说补发和加急 |
| D77 | We're putting new measures in place to prevent this from happening again. | 说预防措施，不说 never |
| D78 | I'm afraid we can't … | 礼貌而清楚地说「不行」 |
| D78 | It's not on our roadmap this year. | 说明产品计划里没有这一项 |
| D78 | … is not something we can do. | 委婉但坚定地说做不到 |
| D78 | What we can do is … | 说完「不行」，马上转到替代方案 |
| D78 | Can we meet you halfway? | 提出各让一步 |
| D78 | My proposal is to … | 提出一个具体的折中做法 |
| D78 | I'll share your suggestion with our product team. | 转达需求，但不替别人答应 |
| D78 | I'll do my best, but I can't promise anything. | 「我尽量」+ 说清不能保证 |
| D79 | I hear you, + 名字. | 对方发火时，先接住情绪 |
| D79 | So, if I understand correctly, + 陈述句 | 复述问题，确认没听错 |
| D79 | That's not acceptable, and I'll make sure + 句子 | 承认问题严重，并承诺结果 |
| D79 | Leave it with me. | 接下问题，让对方放心 |
| D79 | I'll call you back by + 时间. | 给出下一步和最晚时间 |
| D79 | I can ask my manager to join. But I can fix this faster. | 对方要找主管时，给选项、留住问题 |
| D79 | What I can do right now is + 动词原形 | 不说「不是我的错」，只说能做什么 |
| D79 | It's not personal. | 安慰被骂的同事 |
| D80 | Just following up on + 名词. | 礼貌地跟进、催回复 |
| D80 | In case it got buried in your inbox, I've attached it again. | 给对方台阶，顺手重发 |
| D80 | I was wondering if you've had a chance to + 动词原形 | 委婉地问进度 |
| D80 | Any update on + 名词? | 简短地问进展 |
| D80 | Could we touch base on + 时间? | 约一个简短的通话或碰头 |
| D80 | If that doesn't work, I'm happy to reschedule. Just let me know your availability. | 约时间时给对方余地 |
| D80 | Could you … when you get a chance? | 代替 Kindly… ASAP |
| D80 | I'll circle back with … | 答应先去问别人，再回来答复 |
| D81 | I'm sorry to hear about …, and I apologize for … | 来自 D77：同一封邮件里，把「同情」和「认错」分开说（sorry to hear 来自 D27） |
| D81 | According to our policy, … are eligible for … | 来自 D76：引用规定，先说对方能得到什么 |
| D81 | As long as you …, you'll … / Unless …, I'll … | 来自 D76：给希望用 as long as，说例外用 unless，条件里都用一般现在时 |
| D81 | I'm afraid we can't … What we can do is … free of charge. | 来自 D78：清楚地拒绝，马上转到替代方案（free of charge 来自 D75） |
| D81 | … will consider …, but I can't promise anything. | 来自 D78：转达需求，但不替别人答应 |
| D81 | Could we touch base on …? If that doesn't work, I'm happy to reschedule. | 来自 D80：约跟进通话，并给对方改期的余地；跨时区说清 your time |
| D82 | I don't have access to + 系统 / 页面. | 说自己进不去 |
| D82 | Could you give + 人 + access to + 系统? | 替别人或自己申请权限 |
| D82 | He only needs read-only access. | 说明需要的权限范围 |
| D82 | I'd like to request access to… | 正式申请权限 |
| D82 | The code expires in + 时间. | 说明验证码、链接的有效期 |
| D82 | I'm locked out of my account. | 说自己被锁了 |
| D82 | Log out and log back in. | 最常见的 IT 建议 |
| D82 | His access was removed / revoked. | 说权限被收回 |
| D83 | An API is like a waiter between two systems. | 用比喻讲技术概念 |
| D83 | Your system sends a request, and our system sends back the data. | 讲清一来一回 |
| D83 | We can connect your system to ours. / integrate A with B | 说「对接」 |
| D83 | Your data will sync automatically. / The two systems stay in sync. | 说「同步」 |
| D83 | The tracking status updates in real time. | 说实时更新 |
| D83 | We'll test it in the sandbox first. | 先测试再上线 |
| D83 | The integration will go live next month. | 说上线时间 |
| D83 | It all happens behind the scenes. | 告诉非技术人员「这部分你看不到，也不用管」 |
| D84 | The … page keeps + -ing and never loads. | 描述一直存在的故障现象 |
| D84 | It happens every time I … | 说明问题必现、在哪个操作之后出现 |
| D84 | Here are the steps to reproduce the bug. 1. … 2. … 3. … | bug 报告里一步步写出复现方法 |
| D84 | I expected …, but I got … | 对比期望结果和实际结果 |
| D84 | It works fine on my end. Is it working on your end? | 排查时说明自己这边的情况、请对方确认 |
| D84 | Big accounts fail consistently. / The problem is intermittent. | 说明出现的频率和条件 |
| D84 | I'd rate the severity as high, because … | 给出严重程度和理由 |
| D84 | Clearing the cache doesn't help. Two screenshots are attached. | 交代已经试过的办法和附件 |
| D85 | … packages are being held at customs. | 说明包裹此刻的状态（被动进行时） |
| D85 | The address is incomplete, so the delivery attempt failed. | 先说原因、再说结果 |
| D85 | The … is missing. / The zip code was incorrect. | 指出地址具体缺什么、错在哪 |
| D85 | One package was returned to sender. | 说明包裹被退回 |
| D85 | We can reroute it to a pickup point. / The carrier can redeliver it tomorrow. | 给出补救选项 |
| D85 | Our broker is fixing the paperwork for customs clearance. | 说明清关进展、谁在处理 |
| D85 | If an item is prohibited, customs can seize it. | 用一般现在时的条件句解释规则和风险 |
| D85 | Could you let me know which option she prefers? | 请对方在几个选项里做选择 |
| D86 | The outage was detected at …, and service was restored at … | 用过去时 + 被动交代事故的起止时间 |
| D86 | About … percent of orders were affected. | 说明影响范围 |
| D86 | The outage was triggered by … | 说明原因 |
| D86 | As of now, everything is back to normal. | 说明截至某个时间点的状态 |
| D86 | Going forward, we'll … | 承诺具体、能检查的改进措施 |
| D86 | We'll also post live updates on our status page. | 告诉对方去哪儿看最新进展 |
| D86 | That's too much for a customer-facing report. | 区分内部和对外的内容 |
| D86 | One of our lessons learned is that … | 总结教训 |
| D87 | Our working hours only overlap for … | 说两边工作时间的重叠 |
| D87 | I'll send you … by end of day, … time. | 承诺交付时间，并说清时区 |
| D87 | That's … for you. | 帮对方换算时间 |
| D87 | Your team can pick up where we left off. | 交接：对方接着往下做 |
| D87 | I'll … first thing in the morning. | 承诺明天一早优先处理 |
| D87 | For clarity, … | 书面里提醒「说清楚」 |
| D87 | Let's make sure we're on the same page. | 收尾、对齐理解 |
| D87 | Just a heads-up, … / Thanks for the heads-up. | 提前提醒 / 回应提醒 |
| D88 | How does … work? / Here's how it works. | 问流程、开始讲流程 |
| D88 | The customer prints a return label and sends the item back. | 讲退货的第一步 |
| D88 | Every returned item is checked and graded. | 用被动讲「东西被怎么处理」 |
| D88 | … items are restocked and resold. / … are sent back to the manufacturer. | 说不同等级的去向 |
| D88 | Some sellers deduct a … fee from the refund. | 说退货费用 |
| D88 | … can no longer be sold. | 说东西不能再卖、服务不再提供 |
| D88 | For items under …, a return often isn't cost-effective. | 说成本上划不划算 |
| D88 | We offer 30-day returns, no questions asked. | 说退货政策 |
| D89 | Welcome to …! It's a pleasure to have you here. | 欢迎客户来访 |
| D89 | It's great to finally meet you in person. | 第一次见到线上联系很久的人 |
| D89 | It's nice to put a face to the name. | 回应「终于见面」 |
| D89 | Please make yourself at home. | 请客人别客气 |
| D89 | Let me walk you through … | 一步步讲解流程、报告、一天的工作 |
| D89 | Every … is checked by … | 用被动介绍流程（一般现在时） |
| D89 | At nine, I… Then I… In the afternoon, I… At six, I… | 按时间介绍一天的工作 |
| D89 | Thank you for your hospitality. | 客人道谢 |
| D90 | I'm responsible for… / I'm their point of contact. / My main job is to… | 来自 D61、D89：介绍岗位职责 |
| D90 | To begin with, … Next, … Once that's done, … Finally, …（配被动） | 来自 D62、D63、D88、D89：讲一个 5–8 步的流程 |
| D90 | I'm writing to… / Please see below for… / Could you please…? / Best regards, | 来自 D65、D75、D80：写目的清楚的工作邮件 |
| D90 | It has increased to…, up … percentage points. / This was largely due to… / As a result, … | 来自 D68、D69、D72：说数字、趋势和原因 |
| D90 | Just to confirm, …? / That's a good question. I'm not sure off the top of my head. Let me check and get back to you. | 来自 D66、D73：确认理解、被问住时 |
| D90 | I hear you. I'm afraid we can't… What we can do is… | 来自 D78、D79：安抚客户以后礼貌说不、给替代方案 |
| D90 | It happens every time I… I expected…, but… / It's being held at customs. / As of now, … | 来自 D84、D85、D86：说清系统和物流问题 |
| D90 | I've met three of my four objectives. / This quarter, I accomplished… / I'd like to step up and… | 来自 D90：季度总结、绩效面谈 |

# 附录 4 · 第四册衔接记录

- 课程天数：30；重点词 543 个 + 短语 134 个；对比点 124 组；核心表达 231 个；阅读量约 7682 词。

## 已学习的表达模式与中文思维点（第四册不再从零讲解，只在新语境中复现和加深）

- **职责与流程**：be responsible for / point of contact / To begin with… Once that's done… Finally…（Day 61、62）
- **被动语态**：is shipped / has been shipped / is handled by / is being held at customs / was detected（Day 63、64、85、86）
- **工作邮件**：I'm writing to… / Please see below / as mentioned / at your earliest convenience / Just following up on… / I was wondering if…（Day 65、80）
- **确认理解**：Just to confirm… / Do you mean…? / In other words… / What you're saying is…（Day 66）
- **数字与趋势**：percentage point / increase by vs to / on average / out of / account for / year over year（Day 68–70）
- **汇报**：Overall… / pending / at risk / next steps；due to / lead to / as a result；off the top of my head / I'd say / take this offline（Day 71–73）
- **客户沟通**：at the moment / free of charge / according to our policy / as long as / unless / We sincerely apologize / I'm afraid we can't… What we can do is… / I hear you / Leave it with me（Day 75–79）
- **系统与物流**：have access to / read-only / integrate with / in real time / go live / steps to reproduce / on my end / held at customs / return to sender / restocking fee（Day 82–88）

## 已完成的主要情境

- 介绍岗位职责、带新人走流程、给卖家解释订单全过程、弄清「谁管什么」并抄送同事
- 读数字、讲趋势、讲图表、写周报、解释数据变化的原因、在会上回答问题
- 回复咨询、解释赔偿规则、道歉与补救、礼貌拒绝、安抚电话里的愤怒客户、跟进催促
- 开权限、讲 API 对接、写 bug 报告、处理物流异常、写事故说明、跨时区交接、讲退货流程、接待来访客户

## 需要在第四册继续复习的薄弱项目（多数学习者在测评中暴露的问题）

- 被动语态漏掉 be 动词或过去分词（✗ Refunds handle by finance. ✗ It was ship yesterday.）；注意电商里常见的 It has shipped. / Ships in 2 days 是固定说法，自己写邮件时用被动更稳
- percent 和 percentage point 混用；increase by / to 用反
- 邮件开头仍先寒暄、目的放在最后；结尾套话太多
- 拒绝时只说 No / We can't，没有原因和替代方案
- 描述问题太笼统（The system has a problem.），缺少「做了什么 → 期望 → 实际」
- 第二册的完成时 / 过去时和第一册的第三人称 -s 在快速说话时仍会出错

## 第四册应新学的内容

- **商务邮件进阶**：请求、跟进、催促、确认、改期的完整邮件；语气的正式程度
- **会议**：开场、发言、同意与反对、打断与澄清、总结行动项；**转述**他人的话（He said that… / She asked whether…）
- **问题说明**：现象 → 原因 → 影响 → 临时方案 → 长期方案
- **方案沟通与项目跟踪**：比较选项、给出建议、推动决策；条件句（If we…, we could…）
- **词汇**：约 520 个新词 + 140 个短语

## 第四册必须避免的不必要重复

- 前三册附录 1 中已注释的词和短语（第三册 677 个）**不再作为新词完整注释**，只在新语境中复现，并标注首次学习日
- 第三册已讲透的被动语态、数字读法、趋势表达不再单独成课，只作为热身、复习日和易混检查
- Sarah、Daniel、Priya 的场景可以延续，但换成更复杂的协作任务（跨部门项目、需求变更、延期）

# 附录 5 · 续写课程指令

```text
你是一名在中国长期生活、熟悉中美文化的资深英语教育专家和课程设计师。请按照《中文思维桥接英语 · 180 天课程》的既定体系，续写【第四册：Day 91–120 商务协作、邮件、会议、问题说明和方案沟通】。

一、必须沿用的体系（与前三册完全一致）
1. 每天 120 分钟，8 步：旧知识情境复习 10 → 中文意图到英语表达 15 → 微故事与词汇 25 → 语音 15 → 听力 15 → 开口 20 → 输出 12 → 复盘 8（Day 97 / 104 / 111 / 120 为阶段复习日，用复习日的 8 步结构）。每步写明具体操作。
2. 每课结构 A–I：A 今日目标；B 中文场景导入（人物关系、沟通目的、已知信息、表达重点）；C 原创对话 / 邮件 / 会议记录，逐句配自然中文，句子编号 S1、S2…；D 按出现顺序的词汇注释（原文形式、原形、词性、美式 IPA、语境语义、使用场景、1–3 个常用搭配、英文例句 + 中文、中国学习者易错点、位置 S#）；E 3–4 组「中文怎么想 → 英文怎么说」；F 6–8 个核心表达；G 语音与听力；H 口语三层（模仿 → 替换 → 只给中文情境的自主表达）+ 今日输出（口头或书面交替）；I 检测题（前 2 题为听力题）+ 答案 + 完成清单 + 明日复习 + 七日后复习。
3. 每天约 20 个重点词 + 5 个短语，全部自然地出现在当天的文本里；文本从约 240 词逐步增加到约 300 词。
4. 听力：会议式多人对话；只给英文关键词；边听边记笔记；中文只对难句出现。
5. 复习日：用旧词写新情境（复现词标注首次学习日）、中文意思组织英文、短对话听力、不看原文复述、易混表达检查、下阶段改善建议。Day 120 提供阶段成果评估、薄弱项目记录和第五册衔接记录。

二、与前三册的衔接（附件为前三册的 Markdown 源文件，请先读三册的附录 1、2、4）
1. 三册附录 1 中已注释过的词和短语不再作为新词完整注释，只在新语境中复现，并标注首次学习日。
2. 第三册的薄弱项目（被动语态、percent / percentage point、邮件先说目的、拒绝时给原因和替代方案、描述问题要具体）要在第四册的热身、练习和复习日中有计划地反复出现。
3. 继续使用 SwiftLink 的人物：林晓、张涛、Emma、吴佳、李凯、西雅图的 Mike、实习生 Leo，卖家 Sarah、Daniel、Priya；第四册的主线是一个跨部门项目（深圳运营、西雅图工程和美国客户三方协作：延期、系统故障、需求变更）。

三、第四册重点
商务邮件进阶（请求、跟进、催促、确认、改期）、会议（开场、发言、同意与反对、打断与澄清、总结行动项）、转述他人的话、问题说明（现象 → 原因 → 影响 → 临时方案 → 长期方案）、方案沟通与项目跟踪（比较选项、给建议、推动决策、条件句）；约 520 个新词 + 140 个短语。

四、质量要求
所有对话、邮件原创，不复制影视台词或付费教材；不做语法选择题合集；每个知识点都说明「在什么场景用、为什么这样说、中国人容易错在哪」；IPA 准确；不夸大学习效果；联网资源只给能核实的链接，无法核实的要说明。

五、交付
先给出第四册 30 天目录（每天的主题、场景、中英差异点、词汇范围），再逐天写完整教材，最后生成 A4 可打印 PDF 和可编辑源文件，并做完整性检查。

```
